- **ship-reviewed-prs**: Multi-persona pull-request review (senior engineer, senior security, senior infra/SRE/DevOps, senior data engineer, senior frontend engineer, test-coverage signal) with comment-lifecycle suppression and decisive APPROVE/REQUEST_CHANGES/COMMENT submission.
